00303: def meshes(name):
00304:     root=bpy.data.objects.get(name)
00305:     assert root is not None,name
00306:     obs=[o for o in root.children_recursive if o.type=='MESH']
00307:     if root.type=='MESH':
00308:         obs.append(root)
